HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000090/B002","root_000091/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_0bd3d87af74e71751038","text":"The objection to \"new creation\" in 32:10 and the commanded return in 32:11 activated **ب د ء B002**: beginning and return became a deliberate pair. The barren earth producing visible growth in 32:27 later supplied a terrestrial demonstration of good formation from apparently inert material.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000090/B002","root_000091/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5af4fcf99b808115488f","text":"2. **Beginning already contains the prospect of return.** The shared packet ID `ب د ء:B002` has a variant image “beginning paired with return”; the other variant concerns a remarkable first-noticed thing, and it is the first variant that does the work here. The ayah begins human creation, while 32:10 asks about a “new creation,” 32:11 promises return, and 32:12 requests return. **Inferred arrow:** demonstrated capacity to initiate -> capacity to repeat or restore. *Badaʾa khalqa al-insān* therefore becomes the first half of an origin–return argument rather than an isolated prehistory.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_64f5708a388a9edcfaf5","text":"3. **Visible humanity from low material.** **ء ن س B001** gives visible human presence over against hidden beings, while **ط ي ن B001** gives mixed earth and water. The prior ayah opposed unseen and witnessed, and 32:13 later pairs hidden jinn with people. I infer that الإنسان is the manifest, socially present creature produced from terrestrial material. The ayah reads as a transition from hidden divine knowledge to embodied, visible human existence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000323/B001","root_000323/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_650e7ac0d2cf5facc557","text":"1. **Goodness includes skilled making, not appearance alone.** `ح س ن:B002` supplies beneficent or skillful good action, while `خ ل ق:B001` supplies measuring and proportioning and `خ ل ق:B003` supplies complete, balanced form. The universal object “every thing,” followed by the particular sequence clay -> extracted fluid -> completed body and faculties in 32:8–9, gives *aḥsana* a production mechanism. **Inferred arrow:** skillful proportioning of each stage -> the goodness of the created result. The opening clause reads as a claim about well-executed formation and beneficent function, not only visual beauty (`ح س ن:B001`).","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B001","root_000963/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_6621ab622b9248ccd7e0","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"32:7:8\",\"root_000059\",[\"B001\"]],[\"32:7:10\",\"root_000963\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Görünür insanın toprak ve su karışımı maddeden çıkması, görünmeyen bilgiden bedensel ve tanınabilir varoluşa geçişi belirginleştirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000090/B002","root_000091/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_733299cd1593e02bf307","text":"Later 32:10-11 strongly confirmed the first variant of `ب د ء:B002`, beginning paired with return. The initial clay-origin in 32:7 became a functional precedent for renewed creation: complete receipt after death preserves the subject for a second origination rather than leaving “beginning” confined to remote history.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000963/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_97d7770e3a4bf6d05704","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"32:7:10\",\"root_000963\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Balçık, yalnız bir ham madde değil, bedenle birlikte insanın yatkınlıklarının da biçimlendirilebildiği yoğrulabilir bir başlangıç olarak düşünülebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000963/B001","root_000963/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_9b7a40af81266e2985d1","text":"3. **Clay is both mixed material and formed disposition.** `ط ي ن:B001` is earth and water mixed as clay; `ط ي ن:B004` is a molded innate make-up. Nearby 32:8–9 move from material substrate to lineage, bodily completion, and perceptive capacities. **Inferred arrow:** moldable mixed matter -> formed bodily and dispositional organization. The clay phrase reads not as a bare inventory item but as the tractable starting medium of an ordered human form.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000323/B002","root_000434/B001","root_000434/B003","root_000963/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_c1f02c157a9be16713f2","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"32:7:2\",\"root_000323\",[\"B002\"]],[\"32:7:5\",\"root_000434\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]],[\"32:7:7\",\"root_000434\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]],[\"32:7:10\",\"root_000963\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Her şeyi güzel yapmak, düşük değerdeki malzemeyi ölçüp uygun biçime dönüştüren becerikli ve yararlı bir yapım sürecini de anlatır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000090/B001","root_000091/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c633fa646987b0ddd3a5","text":"2. **Beginning already oriented toward return.** **ب د ء B001** supplies opening and initiation, while **ب د ء B002** specifically pairs beginning with return or repetition. Death, transfer, and return follow in 32:10-12. I infer that calling this stage a beginning leaves the process structurally incomplete until return. Human creation from clay is therefore the first movement of a reversible sequence, not a one-time origin isolated from resurrection.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000323/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e0a426c55dfb667cc8d7","text":"1. **Creation as skilled proportioning.** **ح س ن B002** supplies beneficent or skillful good action, **خ ل ق B001-B003** measuring, originating, and well-proportioned form, and **ط ي ن B001** the earth-water material of clay. The relative clause places goodness in the act of making each thing, not only in later evaluation. I infer that \"made well\" names skilled transformation of low material into fitting form. The ayah reads as craftsmanship and proportion, sharpened by the explicit shaping in 32:9.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
